# Skills · M · part 32

827 entries, map-repo to mapping-codebases. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **map-repo**: Use when a repository is to be mapped into the level-aware ontology (application concepts with mappings to shared domain/task concepts and variation values), or when several repositories are analysed for comparison. Produces extraction JSON, never RDF; writes only through the ontologizer write route. ([tohachan/ontologizer](https://github.com/tohachan/ontologizer/tree/HEAD/skills/map-repo))
- **map-reporting-obligations**: Which AML/CTF reports a legal practice may need, incl. suspicious matter reports, tipping-off and privilege. Use for scenarios. For coverage use `$map-designated-services`. Never decides a matter is suspicious or drafts a report. ([l0cka/legal-skills](https://github.com/l0cka/legal-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/legal-skills/skills/map-reporting-obligations) · ★6)
- **map-reports**: Show the owner where things are and what covers what: one generated Leaflet page per site from records (never hand-edited), KML truth that round-trips through Google Earth, 1080x1350 PNGs for the phone thread, install photos filed as evidence, and the Fix-GPS correction loop back into the records. Use when: mapping device positions or coverage, rendering a setup sheet or annotated map image, filing install photos, or accepting a location correction from the owner. ([tikoci/routeros-muse](https://github.com/tikoci/routeros-muse/tree/HEAD/templates/network/.agents/skills/map-reports) · ★1)
- **map-repos**: Builds a read-only, human-confirmed map from your services to their GitHub repositories and writes repo-map.md plus a machine-readable repo-map.json keyed on each repo's immutable numeric id. Use when the user asks to map services to repos, find which repo backs a service, link code to services, or build a service-to-repository map. Never auto-accepts a match; every service is confirmed by the user. Do not use for cluster/traffic topology (use map-topology) or to score anything (use an audit-* skill). ([Scoutflo/ai-readiness](https://github.com/Scoutflo/ai-readiness/tree/HEAD/skills/map-repos) · ★2)
